Author: Jess Linfoot
Date: 2 weeks ago
Rating: 1 star
Absolutely awful experience, avoid this practice at all costs. On attempting to book a face to face appointment online, it was shown to me that the only option available was a phone call appointment, not suitable for this particular issue. Called the practice to attempt to get an in person appointment, and the receptionist was absolutely abhorrent. Condescending, rude and was insistent i book online even though i had repeatedly explained why i could not. After less than a minute long conversation, she had clearly decided that it was not worth her time to book me an appointment and put the phone down on me. As much as i would love to say this is a one off incident, this is now the second time this member of staff has hung up the phone on me. I worry for vulnerable people who may need an appointment urgently and are unable to get one because one member of staff has decided it is not worth her breath to do her job. Worth noting however that all of the doctors and suede practitioners are absolutely lovely and if you can jump through the thousands of hoops to get an appointment they are all very professional, patient and quick.

Author: Parul Singh
Date: 3 weeks ago
Rating: 5 stars
I actually cannot fault this GP practice! I have been with them for around 18 months now, I’ve never struggled for appointment when I’ve needed them, they process my repeat prescriptions same day etc. I’ve seen various GPs at the practice depending on who is available and I have felt heard and supported by every single one, which is so rare in general but particularly as a young, disabled, neurodivergent woman of colour. I had my most recent appointment with Dr Aunet regarding a recent decline in my mental health following a traumatic event. I was struggling to articulate myself because of my mental state but she was kind and let me take my time. I felt my mental health was taken seriously and her priority was ensuring I was safe, then we explored some support options and she offered regular check ins with the GP No GP practice is going to be perfect given the pressure they’re under but that’s not always in their control and I’ve genuinely never had a negative experience and I couldn’t recommend this practice more Thank you to all the team here for restoring my faith in GP practices and doing your best to keep me in good health ❤️

Author: Alexandra Arvatu
Date: 3 weeks ago
Rating: 5 stars
For the first time in the 10 years I’ve been living in the UK, I truly felt listened to and understood during a medical appointment. Dr. Tamara treated me with exceptional care, kindness, and respect. She carefully chose her questions according to my level of understanding, knowing that English is not my first language. That meant so much to me and made me feel comfortable and confident throughout the consultation. She took the time to explain everything clearly and patiently, making sure I understood each step. I never felt rushed or judged. Instead, I felt supported, heard, and genuinely cared for. Her calm and natural manner immediately put me at ease. Dr. Tamara is not only highly professional, but also compassionate and thoughtful. She goes above and beyond to help her patients, and I feel very fortunate to have had her as my doctor. I truly appreciate all the effort she made for me and I am incredibly grateful for her dedication and kindness. She is absolutely amazing.

Author: Ally Wilson

I've attended this surgery for over 20 yrs. I've watched the Practioners, nurses, admin/reception staff come and go. However, I have never felt the need to re-register at another GP practice, once leaving the one I grew up with and joining this one. They work as a team, are exceptionally busy and they have to put up with alot, due to the ever changing society we live in. They can get irritable and frustrated, alittle bit abrupt at times, but they're only human, just like us after all! (and this is only the frontline that I am referring to by the way). (Tip; In my experience a little patience, politeness and gratitude can take you a long way, trust me. Follow the 'Golden Rule'). This may come across to some patients as though they are apathetic to their requirements, their needs, but they work really hard to assist, by making at times the impossible, possible. (Ps note; they can not however "walk on water!)" Just saying.... *Oh yeah, the GP's and other health care Practioners do alright too! 😆
